{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% load static %}

{% block content %}
<div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
    <h1 class="mb-0">Appointment Calendar</h1>
    <a href="{% url 'appointments:add' %}" class="btn btn-primary">
        <i class="fas fa-plus"></i> New Appointment
    </a>
</div>

<div class="card">
    <div class="card-body">
        <div class="table-responsive">
            <table class="table table-hover">
                <thead>
                    <tr>
                        <th>Date</th>
                        <th>Time</th>
                        <th>Patient</th>
                        <th>Service</th>
                        <th>Doctor</th>
                        <th>Status</th>
                        <th>Actions</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                    {% for appointment in appointments %}
                    <tr>
                        <td>{{ appointment.appointment_date|date:"M d, Y" }}</td>
                        <td>{{ appointment.appointment_time|time:"H:i" }}</td>
                        <td>{{ appointment.patient.full_name }}</td>
                        <td>{{ appointment.service.name }}</td>
                        <td>Dr. {{ appointment.doctor.name }}</td>
                        <td>
                            <span class="badge-status {{ appointment.status }}">
                                {{ appointment.get_status_display }}
                            </span>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <div class="btn-group btn-group-sm">
                                <a href="{% url 'appointments:detail' appointment.pk %}" class="btn btn-outline-primary">
                                    <i class="fas fa-eye"></i>
                                </a>
                                <a href="{% url 'appointments:edit' appointment.pk %}" class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                                    <i class="fas fa-edit"></i>
                                </a>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                    {% empty %}
                    <tr>
                        <td colspan="7" class="text-center py-4">
                            <i class="fas fa-calendar fa-2x text-muted mb-2 d-block"></i>
                            <p class="text-muted">No appointments scheduled</p>
                            <a href="{% url 'appointments:add' %}" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">
                                <i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Book First Appointment
                            </a>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                    {% endfor %}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    // Image preview for dental images
    const dentalImagesInput = document.getElementById('dental_images');
    const imagePreviewContainer = document.getElementById('imagePreviewContainer');
    const imagePreviewGrid = document.getElementById('imagePreviewGrid');

    dentalImagesInput.addEventListener('change', function(e) {
        const files = this.files;
        imagePreviewGrid.innerHTML = '';
        
        if (files.length > 0) {
            imagePreviewContainer.style.display = 'block';
            
            Array.from(files).forEach((file, index) => {
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = function(e) {
                    const col = document.createElement('div');
                    col.className = 'col-md-3 col-sm-4 col-6 mb-2';
                    col.innerHTML = `
                        <div style="border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 8px; padding: 5px; text-align: center;">
                            <img src="${e.target.result}" alt="Image ${index + 1}" 
                                 style="width: 100%; height: 100px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 4px;">
                            <small class="d-block text-truncate" style="font-size: 0.7rem;">${file.name}</small>
                            <small class="d-block text-muted" style="font-size: 0.6rem;">${(file.size / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB</small>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    imagePreviewGrid.appendChild(col);
                }
                reader.readAsDataURL(file);
            });
        } else {
            imagePreviewContainer.style.display = 'none';
        }
    });
    
    // Show/hide physician details
    document.querySelectorAll('input[name="under_physician"]').forEach(function(radio) {
        radio.addEventListener('change', function() {
            const wrapper = document.getElementById('physician_details_wrapper');
            if (this.value === 'yes' && this.checked) {
                wrapper.style.display = 'block';
            } else {
                wrapper.style.display = 'none';
            }
        });
    });
    
    // Show/hide discomfort details
    document.querySelectorAll('input[name="dental_discomfort"]').forEach(function(radio) {
        radio.addEventListener('change', function() {
            const wrapper = document.getElementById('discomfort_details_wrapper');
            if (this.value === 'yes' && this.checked) {
                wrapper.style.display = 'block';
            } else {
                wrapper.style.display = 'none';
            }
        });
    });
    
    // Show/hide surgery details
    document.querySelectorAll('input[name="previous_surgery"]').forEach(function(radio) {
        radio.addEventListener('change', function() {
            const wrapper = document.getElementById('surgery_details_wrapper');
            if (this.value === 'yes' && this.checked) {
                wrapper.style.display = 'block';
            } else {
                wrapper.style.display = 'none';
            }
        });
    });
    
    // Set default dates
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
        document.getElementById('date_of_birth').max = today;
        document.getElementById('last_dental_visit').max = today;
        
        // Set default registration date to today
        document.getElementById('registered_at').value = today;
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

    document.getElementById('modalImage').src = url;

        background: #e8f4fd;
        border-left: 4px solid #3498db;
        padding: 10px 15px;
        border-radius: 8px;
        margin-bottom: 15px;
    }
</style>

<script>
    // ==================== LIVE SEARCH ====================
    let searchTimeout;
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('patientSearch');
    const clearSearch = document.getElementById('clearSearch');
    const balanceFilter = document.getElementById('balanceFilter');
    const sortFilter = document.getElementById('sortFilter');
    const tableBody = document.getElementById('patientTableBody');
    const resultCount = document.getElementById('resultCount');
    const searchLoading = document.getElementById('searchLoading');
    
    // âœ… Check if user is doctor (from template context)
    const isDoctor = {{ is_doctor|yesno:"true,false" }};
    
    // Initial result count
    updateResultCount();
    
    // Search on input
    searchInput.addEventListener('input', function() {
        clearTimeout(searchTimeout);
        const query = this.value.trim();
        
        // Show/hide clear button
        if (query.length > 0) {
            clearSearch.style.display = 'block';
        } else {
            clearSearch.style.display = 'none';
        }
        
        // Show loading
        searchLoading.style.display = 'block';
        
        // Debounce search
        searchTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
            performSearch();
        }, 400);
    });
    
    // Clear search
    clearSearch.addEventListener('click', function() {
        searchInput.value = '';
        clearSearch.style.display = 'none';
        performSearch();
        searchInput.focus();
    });
    
    // Filter on change
    balanceFilter.addEventListener('change', function() {
        performSearch();
    });
    
    sortFilter.addEventListener('change', function() {
        performSearch();
    });
    
    // Enter key support
    searchInput.addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            performSearch();
        }
    });
    
    function performSearch() {
        const query = searchInput.value.trim();
        const balance = balanceFilter.value;
        const sort = sortFilter.value;
        
        // Show loading
        searchLoading.style.display = 'block';
        
        // Build URL
        let url = `/patients/api/search/?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&balance=${balance}&sort=${sort}`;
        
        console.log('ðŸ” Searching:', url);
        
        fetch(url)
            .then(response => {
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
                }
                return response.json();
            })
            .then(data => {
                console.log('ðŸ“Š Data received:', data);
                searchLoading.style.display = 'none';
                
                if (data.error) {
                    console.error('âŒ API Error:', data.error);
                    resultCount.textContent = 'Error: ' + data.error;
                    renderEmptyState();
                    return;
                }
                
                if (data.results && data.results.length > 0) {
                    renderResults(data.results);
                    resultCount.textContent = `Showing ${data.results.length} patient${data.results.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
                } else {
                    renderEmptyState();
                    resultCount.textContent = 'No patients found';
                }
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('âŒ Fetch error:', error);
                searchLoading.style.display = 'none';
                resultCount.textContent = 'Error searching patients';
                tableBody.innerHTML = `
                    <tr>
                        <td colspan="6">
                            <div class="text-center no-results text-danger">
                                <i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle fa-3x mb-3"></i>
                                <h5>Search Error</h5>
                                <p class="text-muted">${error.message}</p>
                                <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="performSearch()">
                                    <i class="fas fa-redo"></i> Retry
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            });
    }
    
    function renderResults(patients) {
        tableBody.innerHTML = '';
        
        patients.forEach((patient, index) => {
            const row = document.createElement('tr');
            row.className = 'patient-row';
            row.style.animationDelay = `${index * 0.05}s`;
            
            // Balance badge
            let balanceHtml = '';
            const balance = parseFloat(patient.balance) || 0;
            if (balance > 0) {
                balanceHtml = `<span class="badge bg-danger balance-badge">
                    <i class="fas fa-exclamation-circle"></i> UGX ${balance.toLocaleString()}
                </span>`;
            } else if (balance === 0) {
                balanceHtml = `<span class="badge bg-success balance-badge">
                    <i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> Paid
                </span>`;
            } else {
                balanceHtml = `<span class="badge bg-secondary balance-badge">N/A</span>`;
            }
            
            // Get values with fallbacks
            const fullName = patient.full_name || patient.first_name + ' ' + patient.last_name || 'Unknown';
            const dateOfBirth = patient.date_of_birth || 'N/A';
            const age = patient.age !== undefined && patient.age !== null && patient.age !== 'N/A' ? patient.age + ' years' : 'N/A';
            const phone = patient.phone || 'N/A';
            const email = patient.email || '';
            const registeredAt = patient.registered_at || 'N/A';
            
            // âœ… Build action buttons based on role
            let actionButtons = `
                <a href="/patients/${patient.id}/" class="btn btn-outline-primary quick-action-btn" title="View">
                    <i class="fas fa-eye"></i>
                </a>
            `;
            
            // Only show Edit if NOT a doctor
            if (!isDoctor) {
                actionButtons += `
                <a href="/patients/${patient.id}/edit/" class="btn btn-outline-secondary quick-action-btn" title="Edit">
                    <i class="fas fa-edit"></i>
                </a>
                `;
            }
            
            actionButtons += `
                <a href="/appointments/add/?patient=${patient.id}" class="btn btn-outline-success quick-action-btn" title="Book Appointment">
                    <i class="fas fa-calendar-plus"></i>
                </a>
            `;
            
            if (balance > 0) {
                actionButtons += `
                <a href="/billing/add/?patient=${patient.id}" class="btn btn-outline-warning quick-action-btn" title="Create Invoice">
                    <i class="fas fa-file-invoice"></i>
                </a>
                `;
            }
            
            // Only show Archive if NOT a doctor
            if (!isDoctor) {
                actionButtons += `
                <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline-danger quick-action-btn" title="Archive" 
                        onclick="if(confirm('Archive ${fullName}?')) window.location.href='/patients/${patient.id}/delete/'">
                    <i class="fas fa-archive"></i>
                </button>
                `;
            }
            
            row.innerHTML = `
                <td>
                    <a href="/patients/${patient.id}/" class="fw-bold text-decoration-none">
                        ${fullName}
                    </a>
                    <br>
                    <small class="text-muted">${dateOfBirth}</small>
                    <br>
                    <small class="text-muted"><i class="fas fa-calendar"></i> ${age}</small>
                </td>
                <td>
                    <i class="fas fa-phone text-muted"></i> ${phone}
                </td>
                <td>
                    ${email ? `<i class="fas fa-envelope text-muted"></i> ${email}` : '<span class="text-muted">-</span>'}
                </td>
                <td>${registeredAt}</td>
                <td>${balanceHtml}</td>
                <td>
                    <div class="btn-group btn-group-sm">
                        ${actionButtons}
                    </div>
                </td>
            `;
            
            tableBody.appendChild(row);
        });
    }
    
    function renderEmptyState() {
        tableBody.innerHTML = `
            <tr>
                <td colspan="6">
                    <div class="text-center no-results">
                        <i class="fas fa-search fa-3x text-muted mb-3"></i>
                        <h5>No patients found</h5>
                        <p class="text-muted">Try adjusting your search or filters.</p>
                        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="clearFilters()">
                            <i class="fas fa-undo"></i> Clear Filters
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </td>
            </tr>
        `;
    }
    
    function clearFilters() {
        searchInput.value = '';
        balanceFilter.value = '';
        sortFilter.value = '-registered_at';
        clearSearch.style.display = 'none';
        performSearch();
    }
    
    function updateResultCount() {
        const rows = tableBody.querySelectorAll('tr.patient-row');
        if (rows.length > 0) {
            resultCount.textContent = `Showing ${rows.length} patient${rows.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
        } else {
            resultCount.textContent = '';
        }
    }
    
    // ==================== EXPORT ====================
    document.getElementById('exportBtn').addEventListener('click', function() {
        const rows = tableBody.querySelectorAll('tr.patient-row');
        if (rows.length === 0) {
            alert('No data to export');
            return;
        }
        
        let csv = 'Name,Phone,Email,Registered,Age,Balance\n';
        
        rows.forEach(row => {
            const cells = row.querySelectorAll('td');
            if (cells.length >= 5) {
                const name = cells[0].textContent.trim().split('\n')[0];
                const phone = cells[1].textContent.trim().replace('ðŸ“±', '').trim();
                const email = cells[2].textContent.trim() === '-' ? '' : cells[2].textContent.trim();
                const registered = cells[3].textContent.trim();
                const balance = cells[4].textContent.trim().replace('UGX', '').replace(',', '').trim();
                
                csv += `"${name}","${phone}","${email}","${registered}","${balance}"\n`;
            }
        });
        
        const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv' });
        const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = url;
        a.download = `patients_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.csv`;
        a.click();
        window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

    <div class="filter-card">

                    <div class="col-md-2">
                        <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary w-100">
                            <i class="fas fa-filter"></i> Apply

                    </div>
                    <div class="col-md-2">
                        <a href="{% url 'reports:aging' %}" class="btn btn-secondary w-100">
                            <i class="fas fa-redo"></i> Reset
                        </a>
                    </div>
                    <div class="col-md-2">
                        <span class="badge bg-info p-2 w-100">
                            As of: {{ end_date_display }}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>

    </div>
    
    <!-- Summary Cards -->
    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
        <div class="col-md-3 col-6">
            <div class="stat-card danger">
                <div class="stat-label">Over 90 Days</div>
                <div class="stat-value">UGX {{ aging_data.over_90|floatformat:0|intcomma }}</div>
                <small class="text-muted">{{ aging_data.over_90_count }} invoices</small>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3 col-6">
            <div class="stat-card warning">

<script>
    function exportCSV() {
        var csv = 'Patient,Invoice #,Date,Due Date,Age (Days),Amount,Paid,Balance,Status\n';
        var rows = document.querySelectorAll('.aging-table tbody tr');
        rows.forEach(function(row) {
            var cols = row.querySelectorAll('td');
            if (cols.length > 0) {
                var rowData = [];
                cols.forEach(function(col, index) {
                    if (index !== 8 && index !== 9) { // Skip risk and status columns
                        rowData.push('"' + col.textContent.trim() + '"');
                    }
                });
                // Add status from last column
                var status = cols[9] ? cols[9].textContent.trim() : '';
                rowData.push('"' + status + '"');
                csv += rowData.join(',') + '\n';
            }
        });
        var blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv' });
        var link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        link.download = 'aging_report.csv';
        link.click();
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function exportCSV() {
        var csv = 'Rank,Doctor,Specialization,Revenue,Patients,Appointments,Avg per Patient\n';
        var rows = document.querySelectorAll('.doctor-card');
        var rank = 1;
        rows.forEach(function(card) {
            var name = card.querySelector('.doctor-name')?.textContent || '';
            var spec = card.querySelector('.doctor-specialization')?.textContent || '';
            var metrics = card.querySelectorAll('.metric-box');
            var revenue = metrics[0]?.querySelector('.metric-value')?.textContent || '0';
            var patients = metrics[1]?.querySelector('.metric-value')?.textContent || '0';
            var appointments = metrics[2]?.querySelector('.metric-value')?.textContent || '0';
            var avg = metrics[3]?.querySelector('.metric-value')?.textContent || '0';
            csv += rank + ',"' + name + '","' + spec + '","' + revenue + '","' + patients + '","' + appointments + '","' + avg + '"\n';
            rank++;
        });
        var blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv' });
        var link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        link.download = 'doctor_performance_report.csv';
        link.click();
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

    function exportCSV() {

<style>
    .search-container {
        position: relative;
    }
    .search-results {
        position: absolute;
        top: 100%;
        left: 0;
        right: 0;
        max-height: 300px;

        display: none;
        box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);
    }
    .search-results .result-item {
        padding: 10px 15px;
        cursor: pointer;
        border-bottom: 1px solid #f0f0f0;
        transition: background 0.2s;
    }
    .search-results .result-item:hover {
        background: #f0f8ff;
    }
    .search-results .result-item .name {
        font-weight: 600;
        color: #2c3e50;
    }
    .search-results .result-item .details {
        color: #7f8c8d;
        font-size: 0.9rem;
    }
    .search-results .no-results {
        padding: 20px;
        text-align: center;
        color: #7f8c8d;
    }
    .selected-item {
        background: #f8f9fa;
        border: 2px solid #3498db;
        border-radius: 8px;
        padding: 15px;
        margin-top: 10px;
        display: none;
    }
    .selected-item .item-name {
        font-size: 1.1rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: #2c3e50;
    }
    .selected-item .change-btn {
        cursor: pointer;
        color: #3498db;
        text-decoration: underline;
    }
    .loading-spinner {
        display: none;
        text-align: center;
        padding: 20px;
        color: #7f8c8d;
    }
</style>

<script>
    // ==================== PATIENT SEARCH ====================
    let selectedPatientId = null;
    const patientSearch = document.getElementById('patientSearch');
    const patientResults = document.getElementById('patientResults');
    const patientLoading = document.getElementById('patientLoading');
    const selectedPatient = document.getElementById('selectedPatient');
    const submitBtn = document.getElementById('submitBtn');
    
    // Set default date to today
    document.getElementById('appointment_date').valueAsDate = new Date();
    
    // Patient search
    let patientTimeout;
    patientSearch.addEventListener('input', function() {
        clearTimeout(patientTimeout);
        const query = this.value.trim();
        if (query.length < 2) {
            patientResults.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }
        
        patientLoading.style.display = 'block';
        patientResults.style.display = 'block';
        patientResults.innerHTML = '';
        patientResults.appendChild(patientLoading);
        
        patientTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
            fetch(`/patients/api/search/?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`)
                .then(r => r.json())
                .then(data => {
                    patientLoading.style.display = 'none';
                    patientResults.innerHTML = '';
                    if (data.results && data.results.length > 0) {
                        data.results.forEach(p => {
                            const div = document.createElement('div');
                            div.className = 'result-item';
                            // Use full_name or name
                            const patientName = p.full_name || p.name || 'Unknown';
                            div.innerHTML = `
                                <div class="name">${patientName}</div>
                                <div class="details"><i class="fas fa-phone"></i> ${p.phone || 'N/A'}</div>
                                ${p.email ? `<div class="details"><i class="fas fa-envelope"></i> ${p.email}</div>` : ''}
                            `;
                            div.onclick = () => selectPatient(p.id, patientName, p.phone, p.email);
                            patientResults.appendChild(div);
                        });
                    } else {
                        patientResults.innerHTML = `<div class="no-results"><i class="fas fa-user-slash"></i> No patients found.</div>`;
                    }
                })
                .catch(() => {
                    patientLoading.style.display = 'none';
                    patientResults.innerHTML = '<div class="no-results text-danger">Error searching</div>';
                });
        }, 300);
    });
    
    function selectPatient(id, name, phone, email) {
        selectedPatientId = id;
        document.getElementById('patient').value = id;
        document.getElementById('selectedPatientName').textContent = name;
        document.getElementById('selectedPatientDetails').innerHTML = `
            <i class="fas fa-phone"></i> ${phone || 'N/A'} ${email ? `| <i class="fas fa-envelope"></i> ${email}` : ''}
        `;
        selectedPatient.style.display = 'block';
        patientResults.style.display = 'none';
        patientSearch.value = name;
        
        // Auto-fill notification fields with patient's info
        if (email) {
            document.getElementById('notification_email').placeholder = email;
            document.getElementById('notification_email').title = `Patient's email: ${email}`;
        }
        if (phone) {
            document.getElementById('notification_phone').placeholder = phone;
            document.getElementById('notification_phone').title = `Patient's phone: ${phone}`;
        }
        
        submitBtn.disabled = false;
    }
    
    function clearSelectedPatient() {
        selectedPatientId = null;
        document.getElementById('patient').value = '';
        selectedPatient.style.display = 'none';
        patientSearch.value = '';
        patientSearch.focus();
        submitBtn.disabled = true;
    }
    
    // Clear search results when clicking outside
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        const container = document.querySelector('.search-container');
        if (!container.contains(e.target)) {
            patientResults.style.display = 'none';
        }
    });
    
    document.getElementById('clearPatientSearch').onclick = clearSelectedPatient;
</script>
{% endblock %}

                                    UGX {{ expense.amount|floatformat:0|intcomma }}
                                </strong>
                            </td>
                            <td>{{ expense.get_payment_method_display }}</td>
                            <td>
                                {% if expense.reference_number %}
                                    <span class="badge bg-secondary">{{ expense.reference_number }}</span>
                                {% else %}
                                    <span class="text-muted">-</span>
                                {% endif %}
                            </td>
                            <td>
                                <div class="btn-group btn-group-sm">
                                    <a href="{% url 'billing:expense_edit' expense.pk %}" class="btn btn-outline-primary" title="Edit">
                                        <i class="fas fa-edit"></i>
                                    </a>
                                    <a href="{% url 'billing:expense_delete' expense.pk %}" class="btn btn-outline-danger" title="Delete" 
                                       onclick="return confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this expense?')">
                                        <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                                    </a>
                                    {% if expense.receipt %}
                                    <a href="{{ expense.receipt.url }}" class="btn btn-outline-info" target="_blank" title="View Receipt">
                                        <i class="fas fa-file-pdf"></i>
                                    </a>
                                    {% endif %}
                                </div>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                        {% endfor %}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        {% else %}
            <div class="text-center py-5">
                <i class="fas fa-money-bill-wave fa-3x text-muted mb-3"></i>
                <h5>No expenses recorded</h5>
                <p class="text-muted">Start tracking your clinic expenses.</p>
                <a href="{% url 'billing:expense_add' %}" class="btn btn-primary">
                    <i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Add First Expense
                </a>
            </div>
        {% endif %}
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}

                    <!-- Patient Search -->

            <div class="card-body" id="cartContainer" style="max-height: 500px; overflow-y: auto;">
                <p class="text-muted text-center py-4" id="emptyCart">

<script>
    // ==================== STATE ====================
    let selectedPatientId = null;
    let cartItems = [];
    
    // ==================== DOM Elements ====================
    const patientSearch = document.getElementById('patientSearch');
    const patientResults = document.getElementById('patientResults');
    const patientLoading = document.getElementById('patientLoading');
    const selectedPatient = document.getElementById('selectedPatient');
    const submitBtn = document.getElementById('submitBtn');
    
    const itemSearch = document.getElementById('itemSearch');
    const itemResults = document.getElementById('itemResults');
    const itemLoading = document.getElementById('itemLoading');
    const itemQuantity = document.getElementById('itemQuantity');
    const itemPrice = document.getElementById('itemPrice');
    const addItemBtn = document.getElementById('addItemBtn');
    
    const cartContainer = document.getElementById('cartContainer');
    const itemCount = document.getElementById('itemCount');
    const cartTotal = document.getElementById('cartTotal');
    const cartItemsHidden = document.getElementById('cartItemsHidden');
    const subtotalDisplay = document.getElementById('subtotalDisplay');
    const subtotalHidden = document.getElementById('subtotalHidden');
    const taxRate = document.getElementById('taxRate');
    const discount = document.getElementById('discount');
    const dueDate = document.getElementById('dueDate');
    const issueDate = document.getElementById('issueDate');
    
    // ==================== INIT ====================
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
        const dueDateValue = new Date();
        dueDateValue.setDate(dueDateValue.getDate() + 30);
        
        // Set default issue date to today
        issueDate.value = today;
        
        // Set default due date to 30 days from now
        dueDate.value = dueDateValue.toISOString().split('T')[0];
    });
    
    // ==================== UPDATE HIDDEN FIELD ====================
    function updateCartHiddenField() {
        const cartData = JSON.stringify(cartItems);
        document.getElementById('cartItemsHidden').value = cartData;
    }
    
    // ==================== PATIENT SEARCH ====================
    let patientTimeout;
    patientSearch.addEventListener('input', function() {
        clearTimeout(patientTimeout);
        const query = this.value.trim();
        if (query.length < 2) {
            patientResults.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }
        
        patientLoading.style.display = 'block';
        patientResults.style.display = 'block';
        patientResults.innerHTML = '';
        patientResults.appendChild(patientLoading);
        
        patientTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
            fetch(`/patients/api/search/?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`)
                .then(r => r.json())
                .then(data => {
                    patientLoading.style.display = 'none';
                    patientResults.innerHTML = '';
                    if (data.results && data.results.length > 0) {
                        data.results.forEach(p => {
                            const div = document.createElement('div');
                            div.className = 'result-item';
                            const patientName = p.full_name || p.name || 'Unknown';
                            div.innerHTML = `
                                <div class="name">${patientName}</div>
                                <div class="details"><i class="fas fa-phone"></i> ${p.phone || 'N/A'}</div>
                                ${p.email ? `<div class="details"><i class="fas fa-envelope"></i> ${p.email}</div>` : ''}
                            `;
                            div.onclick = () => selectPatient(p.id, patientName, p.phone, p.email);
                            patientResults.appendChild(div);
                        });
                    } else {
                        patientResults.innerHTML = `<div class="no-results"><i class="fas fa-user-slash"></i> No patients found.</div>`;
                    }
                })
                .catch(() => {
                    patientLoading.style.display = 'none';
                    patientResults.innerHTML = '<div class="no-results text-danger">Error searching</div>';
                });
        }, 300);
    });
    
    function selectPatient(id, name, phone, email) {
        selectedPatientId = id;
        document.getElementById('patient').value = id;
        document.getElementById('selectedPatientName').textContent = name;
        document.getElementById('selectedPatientDetails').innerHTML = `
            <i class="fas fa-phone"></i> ${phone || 'N/A'} ${email ? `| <i class="fas fa-envelope"></i> ${email}` : ''}
        `;
        selectedPatient.style.display = 'block';
        patientResults.style.display = 'none';
        patientSearch.value = name;
        checkSubmit();
    }
    
    function clearSelectedPatient() {
        selectedPatientId = null;
        document.getElementById('patient').value = '';
        selectedPatient.style.display = 'none';
        patientSearch.value = '';
        patientSearch.focus();
        checkSubmit();
    }
    
    // ==================== ITEM SEARCH ====================
    let itemTimeout;
    itemSearch.addEventListener('input', function() {
        clearTimeout(itemTimeout);
        const query = this.value.trim();
        if (query.length < 2) {
            itemResults.style.display = 'none';
            addItemBtn.disabled = true;
            return;
        }
        
        itemLoading.style.display = 'block';
        itemResults.style.display = 'block';
        itemResults.innerHTML = '';
        itemResults.appendChild(itemLoading);
        addItemBtn.disabled = true;
        
        itemTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
            Promise.all([
                fetch(`/appointments/api/services/search/?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({results: []})),
                fetch(`/inventory/api/search/?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({results: []}))
            ])
            .then(([services, inventory]) => {
                itemLoading.style.display = 'none';
                itemResults.innerHTML = '';
                
                let results = [];
                
                if (services.results && services.results.length > 0) {
                    services.results.forEach(s => {
                        results.push({
                            id: s.id,
                            name: s.name,
                            price: s.price,
                            type: 'service',
                            typeLabel: 'Service',
                            stock: null,
                            unit: null
                        });
                    });
                }
                
                if (inventory.results && inventory.results.length > 0) {
                    inventory.results.forEach(i => {
                        results.push({
                            id: i.id,
                            name: i.name,
                            price: i.selling_price,
                            type: 'inventory',
                            typeLabel: 'Supply',
                            stock: i.quantity,
                            unit: i.unit
                        });
                    });
                }
                
                if (results.length > 0) {
                    results.forEach(item => {
                        const div = document.createElement('div');
                        div.className = 'result-item';
                        const inCart = cartItems.some(c => c.id === item.id && c.type === item.type);
                        div.innerHTML = `
                            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                                <div>
                                    <div class="name">
                                        ${item.name}
                                        <span class="badge ${item.type === 'service' ? 'bg-primary' : 'bg-success'}">${item.typeLabel}</span>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="details">
                                        ${item.type === 'inventory' ? `<i class="fas fa-boxes"></i> Stock: ${item.stock} ${item.unit} | ` : ''}
                                        <i class="fas fa-tag"></i> UGX ${Number(item.price).toLocaleString()}
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                ${inCart ? 
                                    '<span class="badge bg-success"><i class="fas fa-check"></i> Added</span>' :
                                    `<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-success" onclick="selectItemForCart(${item.id}, '${item.name.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', ${item.price}, '${item.type}', ${item.stock || null}, '${item.unit || ''}')">
                                        <i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Select
                                    </button>`
                                }
                            </div>
                        `;
                        itemResults.appendChild(div);
                    });
                    addItemBtn.disabled = false;
                } else {
                    itemResults.innerHTML = '<div class="no-results"><i class="fas fa-box-open"></i> No items found.</div>';
                    addItemBtn.disabled = true;
                }
            })
            .catch(() => {
                itemLoading.style.display = 'none';
                itemResults.innerHTML = '<div class="no-results text-danger">Error searching. Please try again.</div>';
                addItemBtn.disabled = true;
            });
        }, 400);
    });
    
    // ==================== SELECT ITEM ====================
    let selectedItemData = null;
    
    function selectItemForCart(id, name, price, type, stock, unit) {
        selectedItemData = {
            id: id,
            name: name,
            price: price,
            type: type,
            stock: stock,
            unit: unit
        };
        
        document.getElementById('itemPrice').value = price;
        document.getElementById('itemQuantity').focus();
        
        itemResults.innerHTML = `
            <div class="result-item" style="background: #e8f5e9;">
                <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                    <div>
                        <div class="name">
                            ${name}
                            <span class="badge ${type === 'service' ? 'bg-primary' : 'bg-success'}">${type === 'service' ? 'Service' : 'Supply'}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="details">
                            ${type === 'inventory' ? `<i class="fas fa-boxes"></i> Stock: ${stock} ${unit} | ` : ''}
                            <i class="fas fa-tag"></i> UGX ${Number(price).toLocaleString()}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <span class="badge bg-info">Selected</span>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
        
        addItemBtn.disabled = false;
    }
    
    // ==================== ADD ITEMS ====================
    function addItemToCart() {
        if (!selectedItemData) {
            alert('Please search and select an item first.');
            return;
        }
        
        const id = selectedItemData.id;
        const name = selectedItemData.name;
        const type = selectedItemData.type;
        const stock = selectedItemData.stock;
        const unit = selectedItemData.unit;
        const price = parseFloat(document.getElementById('itemPrice').value) || 0;
        const quantity = parseInt(document.getElementById('itemQuantity').value) || 1;
        
        if (price <= 0) {
            alert('Please enter a valid price.');
            return;
        }
        
        if (quantity < 1) {
            alert('Quantity must be at least 1.');
            return;
        }
        
        if (type === 'inventory' && stock !== null && quantity > stock) {
            alert(`Only ${stock} items available in stock!`);
            return;
        }
        
        if (cartItems.some(c => c.id === id && c.type === type)) {
            alert('Item already in cart!');
            return;
        }
        
        const newItem = {
            id: id,
            name: name,
            price: price,
            type: type,
            typeLabel: type === 'service' ? 'Service' : 'Supply',
            quantity: quantity,
            stock: stock,
            unit: unit || ''
        };
        
        cartItems.push(newItem);
        
        renderCart();
        updateTotals();
        checkSubmit();
        updateCartHiddenField();
        
        itemSearch.value = '';
        itemResults.style.display = 'none';
        itemResults.innerHTML = '';
        itemQuantity.value = 1;
        itemPrice.value = 0;
        addItemBtn.disabled = true;
        selectedItemData = null;
        
        storeCartInSession();
    }
    
    function quickAddService(id, name, price) {
        if (cartItems.some(c => c.id === id && c.type === 'service')) {
            alert('Service already in cart!');
            return;
        }
        
        const quantity = parseInt(document.getElementById('itemQuantity').value) || 1;
        
        cartItems.push({
            id: id,
            name: name,
            price: price,
            type: 'service',
            typeLabel: 'Service',
            quantity: quantity,
            stock: null,
            unit: ''
        });
        
        renderCart();
        updateTotals();
        checkSubmit();
        updateCartHiddenField();
        storeCartInSession();
    }
    
    function removeFromCart(index) {
        cartItems.splice(index, 1);
        renderCart();
        updateTotals();
        checkSubmit();
        updateCartHiddenField();
        storeCartInSession();
    }
    
    function updateQuantity(index, newQty) {
        if (newQty < 1) { removeFromCart(index); return; }
        const item = cartItems[index];
        if (item.type === 'inventory' && item.stock !== null && newQty > item.stock) {
            alert(`Only ${item.stock} items available in stock!`);
            return;
        }
        item.quantity = newQty;
        renderCart();
        updateTotals();
        updateCartHiddenField();
        storeCartInSession();
    }
    
    function updatePrice(index, newPrice) {
        if (newPrice < 0) { return; }
        const item = cartItems[index];
        item.price = newPrice;
        renderCart();
        updateTotals();
        updateCartHiddenField();
        storeCartInSession();
    }
    
    // ==================== STORE CART IN SESSION ====================
    function storeCartInSession() {
        const cartData = JSON.stringify(cartItems);
        fetch('/billing/store-cart/', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRFToken': document.querySelector('[name=csrfmiddlewaretoken]').value
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({ cart_items: cartData })
        }).catch(error => console.error('Error storing cart:', error));
    }
    
    // ==================== RENDER CART ====================
    function renderCart() {
        cartContainer.innerHTML = '';
        if (cartItems.length === 0) {
            cartContainer.innerHTML = `
                <p class="text-muted text-center py-4">
                    <i class="fas fa-cart-plus fa-2x d-block mb-2"></i>
                    No items added yet.<br>
                    Search and add items above.
                </p>
            `;
            itemCount.textContent = '0';
            return;
        }
        
        cartItems.forEach((item, index) => {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'cart-item';
            div.innerHTML = `
                <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-start">
                    <div>
                        <div>
                            <span class="item-type-badge ${item.type}">${item.typeLabel}</span>
                            <span class="fw-bold">${item.name}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="small text-muted">
                            ${item.type === 'inventory' ? `Stock: ${item.stock} ${item.unit} | ` : ''}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" onclick="removeFromCart(${index})">
                        <i class="fas fa-times"></i>
                    </button>
                </div>
                <div class="row mt-2">
                    <div class="col-4">
                        <label class="form-label small">Qty</label>
                        <input type="number" class="form-control form-control-sm" 
                               value="${item.quantity}" min="1" 
                               ${item.type === 'inventory' && item.stock !== null ? `max="${item.stock}"` : ''}
                               onchange="updateQuantity(${index}, parseInt(this.value))">
                    </div>
                    <div class="col-4">
                        <label class="form-label small">Price</label>
                        <input type="number" step="0.01" class="form-control form-control-sm" 
                               value="${item.price}" min="0"
                               onchange="updatePrice(${index}, parseFloat(this.value))">
                    </div>
                    <div class="col-4 text-end">
                        <label class="form-label small">Total</label>
                        <div class="fw-bold text-success">UGX ${(item.price * item.quantity).toLocaleString()}</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
            cartContainer.appendChild(div);
        });
        
        itemCount.textContent = cartItems.length;
    }
    
    // ==================== TOTALS ====================
    function updateTotals() {
        const subtotal = cartItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * item.quantity), 0);
        const taxRateVal = parseFloat(taxRate.value) || 0;
        const discountVal = parseFloat(discount.value) || 0;
        const taxAmount = (subtotal * taxRateVal) / 100;
        const total = subtotal + taxAmount - discountVal;
        
        subtotalDisplay.value = `UGX ${subtotal.toLocaleString()}`;
        subtotalHidden.value = subtotal;
        cartTotal.textContent = `UGX ${total.toLocaleString()}`;
    }
    
    // ==================== CHECK SUBMIT ====================
    function checkSubmit() {
        const hasPatient = selectedPatientId !== null;
        const hasItems = cartItems.length > 0;
        submitBtn.disabled = !(hasPatient && hasItems);
    }
    
    // ==================== SUBMIT INVOICE ====================
    function submitInvoice() {
        if (cartItems.length === 0) {
            alert('Please add at least one item to the invoice.');
            return;
        }
        
        if (!selectedPatientId) {
            alert('Please select a patient.');
            return;
        }
        
        const cartData = JSON.stringify(cartItems);
        document.getElementById('cartItemsHidden').value = cartData;
        
        document.getElementById('invoiceForm').submit();
    }
    
    // ==================== EVENTS ====================
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.search-container .search-results').forEach(el => {
            if (!el.parentElement.contains(e.target)) {
                el.style.display = 'none';
            }
        });
    });
    
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
            document.querySelectorAll('.search-results').forEach(el => el.style.display = 'none');
        }
    });
    
    document.getElementById('clearPatientSearch').onclick = clearSelectedPatient;
    
    addItemBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        addItemToCart();
    });
    
    document.getElementById('itemPrice').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            addItemToCart();
        }
    });
    
    document.getElementById('itemQuantity').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            addItemToCart();
        }
    });
    
    itemSearch.addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            const firstSelectBtn = itemResults.querySelector('.btn-success');
            if (firstSelectBtn) {
                firstSelectBtn.click();
            }
        }
    });
    
    document.getElementById('invoiceForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        submitInvoice();
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function toggleItemType() {
        const type = document.getElementById('itemType').value;
        document.getElementById('serviceFields').style.display = type === 'service' ? 'block' : 'none';
        document.getElementById('inventoryFields').style.display = type === 'inventory' ? 'block' : 'none';
        document.getElementById('customFields').style.display = type === 'custom' ? 'block' : 'none';
    }
    
    function updateInventoryPrice() {
        const select = document.getElementById('inventorySelect');
        const selected = select.options[select.selectedIndex];
        if (selected && selected.dataset.price) {
            document.getElementById('unitPrice').value = selected.dataset.price;
        }
    }
</script>

<script>
    function exportCSV() {
        var csv = 'Invoice #,Patient,Date,Total,Paid,Balance,Status,Payment Method\n';
        var rows = document.querySelectorAll('.table-invoices tbody tr');
        rows.forEach(function(row) {
            var cols = row.querySelectorAll('td');
            if (cols.length > 0) {
                var rowData = [];
                cols.forEach(function(col) {
                    rowData.push('"' + col.textContent.trim() + '"');
                });
                csv += rowData.join(',') + '\n';
            }
        });
        var blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv' });
        var link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        link.download = 'invoices.csv';
        link.click();
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% extends 'core/base_otp.html' %}
{% load static %}

{% block title %}OTP Verification{% endblock %}

{% block extra_css %}
<style>
    .otp-input {
        text-align: center;
        font-size: 2rem;
        font-weight: bold;
        letter-spacing: 10px;
        padding: 15px;
        border-radius: 12px;
        border: 2px solid #dee2e6;
        transition: all 0.3s;
    }
    .otp-input:focus {
        border-color: #667eea;
        box-shadow: 0 0 0 0.2rem rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.25);
    }
    .resend-link {
        color: #667eea;
        text-decoration: underline;
        cursor: pointer;
    }
    .resend-link:hover {
        color: #764ba2;
    }
    .timer {
        font-weight: 600;
        color: #e74c3c;
    }
    .otp-expired {
        color: #e74c3c;
        font-weight: 600;
    }
</style>
{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<div class="otp-title">OTP Verification</div>
<div class="otp-subtitle">Enter the code sent to your phone</div>

{% if messages %}
    {% for message in messages %}
        <div class="alert alert-{{ message.tags }} alert-dismissible fade show" role="alert">
            {{ message }}
            <button type="button" class="btn-close" data-bs-dismiss="alert"></button>
        </div>
    {% endfor %}
{% endif %}

<div class="text-center mb-4">
    <i class="fas fa-sms fa-3x text-primary"></i>
    <p class="text-muted mt-2">
        Enter the 6-digit code sent to:<br>
        <strong>{{ user.profile.phone }}</strong>
    </p>
    <p class="text-muted small" id="otpTimer">
        <i class="fas fa-clock"></i> 
        OTP expires in: <span class="timer" id="countdown">5:00</span>
    </p>
    {% if not user.profile.otp_code %}
        <p class="text-warning">
            <i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> 
            No OTP has been sent. Please request a new one.
        </p>
    {% endif %}
</div>

<form method="post">
    {% csrf_token %}
    <div class="mb-4">
        <label for="otp_code" class="form-label">Enter OTP Code</label>
        <input type="text" class="form-control otp-input" id="otp_code" 
               name="otp_code" placeholder="000000" maxlength="6" 
               inputmode="numeric" pattern="[0-9]*" required autofocus
               {% if not user.profile.otp_code %}disabled{% endif %}>
    </div>
    
    <button type="submit" class="btn-verify" 
            {% if not user.profile.otp_code %}disabled{% endif %}>
        <i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> Verify OTP
    </button>
</form>

<div class="text-center mt-3">
    <small>
        Didn't receive the code?
        <a href="{% url 'core:otp_send' %}" class="resend-link" id="resendLink">
            <i class="fas fa-redo"></i> Resend OTP
        </a>
    </small>
</div>

<div class="text-center mt-2">
    <small>
        <a href="{% url 'core:logout' %}" class="text-danger">
            <i class="fas fa-sign-out-alt"></i> Logout
        </a>
    </small>
</div>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    // Countdown timer for OTP expiry
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        // Only start timer if OTP exists
        if ({{ user.profile.otp_code|yesno:"true,false" }}) {
            startTimer(5); // 5 minutes
        }
        
        // Auto-submit when 6 digits are entered
        const otpInput = document.getElementById('otp_code');
        otpInput.addEventListener('input', function() {
            this.value = this.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
            if (this.value.length === 6) {
                this.form.submit();
            }
        });
        otpInput.focus();
    });
    
    function startTimer(minutes) {
        let time = minutes * 60; // seconds
        const timerDisplay = document.getElementById('countdown');
        const resendLink = document.getElementById('resendLink');
        
        const interval = setInterval(function() {
            const mins = Math.floor(time / 60);
            const secs = time % 60;
            timerDisplay.textContent = `${mins}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
            
            if (time <= 0) {
                clearInterval(interval);
                timerDisplay.textContent = 'Expired!';
                timerDisplay.className = 'timer otp-expired';
                resendLink.style.color = '#e74c3c';
                resendLink.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> OTP Expired - Click to Resend';
                // Disable verify button
                document.querySelector('button[type="submit"]').disabled = true;
                document.getElementById('otp_code').disabled = true;
            }
            time--;
        }, 1000);
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function toggleDoctorFields() {
        const role = document.getElementById('role').value;
        const doctorFields = document.getElementById('doctorFields');
        if (role === 'doctor') {
            doctorFields.style.display = 'block';
        } else {
            doctorFields.style.display = 'none';
        }
    }
    
    function toggleDoctorLink() {
        const option = document.getElementById('doctor_option').value;
        const linkField = document.getElementById('doctorLinkField');
        if (option === 'existing') {
            linkField.style.display = 'block';
        } else {
            linkField.style.display = 'none';
        }
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
function sendYoolaTest() {
    const phone = document.getElementById('test_phone').value;
    const message = document.getElementById('test_message').value;
    
    if (!phone) {
        alert('Please enter a phone number');
        return;
    }
    
    if (!message) {
        alert('Please enter a message');
        return;
    }
    
    if (confirm(`Send test SMS to ${phone}?`)) {
        const form = document.createElement('form');
        form.method = 'POST';
        form.action = "{% url 'notifications:test_yoola' %}";
        
        const csrf = document.createElement('input');
        csrf.type = 'hidden';
        csrf.name = 'csrfmiddlewaretoken';
        csrf.value = document.querySelector('[name=csrfmiddlewaretoken]').value;
        form.appendChild(csrf);
        
        const phoneInput = document.createElement('input');
        phoneInput.type = 'hidden';
        phoneInput.name = 'phone_number';
        phoneInput.value = phone;
        form.appendChild(phoneInput);
        
        const messageInput = document.createElement('input');
        messageInput.type = 'hidden';
        messageInput.name = 'message';
        messageInput.value = message;
        form.appendChild(messageInput);
        
        document.body.appendChild(form);
        form.submit();
    }
}
</script>

<script>
    function sendReminders() {
        const days = document.getElementById('daysSelect').value;
        const form = document.getElementById('reminderForm');
        
        if (confirm(`Send reminders for appointments in ${days} day(s)?`)) {
            form.submit();
        }
    }
    
    function sendSingleReminder(appointmentId) {
        if (confirm('Send reminder for this appointment?')) {
            fetch(`/notifications/send-single-reminder/${appointmentId}/`, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'X-CSRFToken': document.querySelector('[name=csrfmiddlewaretoken]').value,
                },
            })
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.success) {
                    alert('âœ… Reminder sent successfully!');
                    location.reload();
                } else {
                    alert('âŒ Failed to send reminder: ' + data.error);
                }
            })
            .catch(error => {
                alert('âŒ Error: ' + error);
            });
        }
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

                        <label for="image_description" class="form-label">Image Description (Optional)</label>
                        <textarea class="form-control" id="image_description" name="image_description" rows="2" 
                                  placeholder="Describe what the images show (e.g., 'Upper right molar cavity', 'Before treatment - tooth 23')"></textarea>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between">
                        <a href="{% url 'patients:detail' patient.pk %}" class="btn btn-secondary">
                            <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Cancel
                        </a>
                        <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary" id="uploadBtn">
                            <i class="fas fa-upload"></i> Upload Images
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<script>
    // Drag and drop functionality
    const dropZone = document.getElementById('dropZone');
    const fileInput = document.getElementById('dental_images');
    const previewContainer = document.getElementById('previewContainer');
    const previewText = document.getElementById('previewText');
    const uploadBtn = document.getElementById('uploadBtn');
    let selectedFiles = [];

    // Click to browse
    dropZone.addEventListener('click', function() {
        fileInput.click();
    });

    // File selection
    fileInput.addEventListener('change', function(e) {
        handleFiles(this.files);
    });

    // Drag and drop events
    dropZone.addEventListener('dragover', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        this.style.borderColor = '#667eea';
        this.style.background = '#f0f4ff';
    });

    dropZone.addEventListener('dragleave', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        this.style.borderColor = '#dee2e6';
        this.style.background = 'transparent';
    });

    dropZone.addEventListener('drop', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        this.style.borderColor = '#dee2e6';
        this.style.background = 'transparent';
        handleFiles(e.dataTransfer.files);
    });

    function handleFiles(files) {
        // Convert FileList to array
        const newFiles = Array.from(files);
        selectedFiles = [...selectedFiles, ...newFiles];
        
        // Update the file input
        const dt = new DataTransfer();
        selectedFiles.forEach(file => dt.items.add(file));
        fileInput.files = dt.files;
        
        // Update preview
        renderPreviews();
        
        // Hide placeholder text
        previewText.style.display = selectedFiles.length > 0 ? 'none' : 'block';
    }

    function renderPreviews() {
        previewContainer.innerHTML = '';
        
        selectedFiles.forEach((file, index) => {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function(e) {
                const div = document.createElement('div');
                div.className = 'preview-item';
                div.innerHTML = `
                    <img src="${e.target.result}" alt="${file.name}">
                    <div class="file-name">${file.name}</div>
                    <button class="remove-btn" onclick="removeFile(${index})">Ã—</button>
                `;
                previewContainer.appendChild(div);
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        });
        
        // Enable/disable upload button
        uploadBtn.disabled = selectedFiles.length === 0;
    }

    function removeFile(index) {
        selectedFiles.splice(index, 1);
        
        // Update file input
        const dt = new DataTransfer();
        selectedFiles.forEach(file => dt.items.add(file));
        fileInput.files = dt.files;
        
        renderPreviews();
        previewText.style.display = selectedFiles.length > 0 ? 'none' : 'block';
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function openToothModal(patientId, toothNumber, currentCondition, currentSurface, currentNotes) {
        document.getElementById('toothNumber').value = toothNumber;
        document.getElementById('condition').value = currentCondition || '';
        document.getElementById('surface').value = currentSurface || '';
        document.getElementById('notes').value = currentNotes || '';
        
        // Update tooth name
        const toothNames = {
            1: 'Right Maxillary 3rd Molar', 2: 'Right Maxillary 2nd Molar',
            3: 'Right Maxillary 1st Molar', 4: 'Right Maxillary 2nd Premolar',
            5: 'Right Maxillary 1st Premolar', 6: 'Right Maxillary Canine',
            7: 'Right Maxillary Lateral Incisor', 8: 'Right Maxillary Central Incisor',
            9: 'Left Maxillary Central Incisor', 10: 'Left Maxillary Lateral Incisor',
            11: 'Left Maxillary Canine', 12: 'Left Maxillary 1st Premolar',
            13: 'Left Maxillary 2nd Premolar', 14: 'Left Maxillary 1st Molar',
            15: 'Left Maxillary 2nd Molar', 16: 'Left Maxillary 3rd Molar',
            17: 'Left Mandibular 3rd Molar', 18: 'Left Mandibular 2nd Molar',
            19: 'Left Mandibular 1st Molar', 20: 'Left Mandibular 2nd Premolar',
            21: 'Left Mandibular 1st Premolar', 22: 'Left Mandibular Canine',
            23: 'Left Mandibular Lateral Incisor', 24: 'Left Mandibular Central Incisor',
            25: 'Right Mandibular Central Incisor', 26: 'Right Mandibular Lateral Incisor',
            27: 'Right Mandibular Canine', 28: 'Right Mandibular 1st Premolar',
            29: 'Right Mandibular 2nd Premolar', 30: 'Right Mandibular 1st Molar',
            31: 'Right Mandibular 2nd Molar', 32: 'Right Mandibular 3rd Molar'
        };
        
        document.getElementById('toothNameDisplay').textContent = `Tooth #${toothNumber} - ${toothNames[toothNumber] || 'Unknown'}`;
        
        // Update preview color based on condition
        const preview = document.getElementById('toothPreview');
        const colors = {
            'healthy': { bg: '#e8f5e9', border: '#00b894' },
            'decay': { bg: '#fce4ec', border: '#e74c3c' },
            'filling': { bg: '#fff3e0', border: '#fdcb6e' },
            'crown': { bg: '#f3e5f5', border: '#6c5ce7' },
            'bridge': { bg: '#e3f2fd', border: '#0984e3' },
            'extraction': { bg: '#f5f5f5', border: '#e74c3c' },
            'implant': { bg: '#e0f2f1', border: '#00b894' },
            'root_canal': { bg: '#fce4ec', border: '#e17055' },
            'missing': { bg: '#f5f5f5', border: '#b2bec3' },
        };
        
        const color = colors[currentCondition] || { bg: '#e8f5e9', border: '#00b894' };
        preview.style.background = color.bg;
        preview.style.border = `2px solid ${color.border}`;
        
        document.getElementById('toothForm').action = `/patients/${patientId}/dental-chart/`;
        
        const modal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('toothModal'));
        modal.show();
    }
</script>

<script>
    function applyFilters() {
        const status = document.getElementById('statusFilter').value;
        const date = document.getElementById('dateFilter').value;
        let url = "{% url 'patient_portal:appointments' %}?";
        if (status) url += "status=" + status + "&";
        if (date) url += "date=" + date + "&";
        window.location.href = url;
    }
    
    function clearFilters() {
        window.location.href = "{% url 'patient_portal:appointments' %}";
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

            </div>

            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

<script>
    function openImageModal(url, description, type, date) {
        document.getElementById('modalImage').src = url;
        document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = type;
        document.getElementById('modalDescription').textContent = description;
        document.getElementById('modalDate').textContent = date;
    }
</script>

<script>
    function exportCSV() {
        var csv = 'Patient,Invoice #,Date,Due Date,Age (Days),Amount,Paid,Balance,Status\n';
        var rows = document.querySelectorAll('.aging-table tbody tr');
        rows.forEach(function(row) {
            var cols = row.querySelectorAll('td');
            if (cols.length > 0) {
                var rowData = [];
                cols.forEach(function(col, index) {
                    if (index !== 8 && index !== 9) { // Skip risk and status columns
                        rowData.push('"' + col.textContent.trim() + '"');
                    }
                });
                // Add status from last column
                var status = cols[9] ? cols[9].textContent.trim() : '';
                rowData.push('"' + status + '"');
                csv += rowData.join(',') + '\n';
            }
        });
        var blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv' });
        var link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        link.download = 'aging_report.csv';
        link.click();
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function exportCSV() {
        var csv = 'Patient,Phone,Gender,Visits,Total Spent,Avg per Visit,First Visit,Last Visit\n';
        var rows = document.querySelectorAll('.visit-table tbody tr');
        rows.forEach(function(row) {
            var cols = row.querySelectorAll('td');
            if (cols.length > 0) {
                var rowData = [];
                cols.forEach(function(col) {
                    rowData.push('"' + col.textContent.trim() + '"');
                });
                csv += rowData.join(',') + '\n';
            }
        });
        var blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv' });
        var link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        link.download = 'patient_visits_report.csv';
        link.click();
    }
</script>
